# Third-party notices

Gilde Code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (`LICENSE`, `NOTICE`).
A built `Gilde Code.app` also contains the third-party software listed here. This
file ships inside the app at `Contents/Resources/licenses/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`.

Generated by `node scripts/third-party-notices.mjs` from `src-tauri/Cargo.lock` (target
`aarch64-apple-darwin`, dev-dependencies excluded), `package-lock.json` and
`src-tauri/sidecar/package-lock.json` (dev-dependencies excluded). Do not edit by hand;
`node scripts/third-party-notices.mjs --check` fails when it is out of date.

## Node.js runtime

The app bundles the official Node.js v20.19.2 `darwin-arm64` binary as
`Contents/MacOS/gilde-node`. Node.js is licensed under the MIT license; its full
LICENSE, including the notices of the components it links statically (V8, OpenSSL,
ICU, libuv, and others), ships unmodified at
`Contents/Resources/licenses/node-LICENSE`.
